For embarrassment, the bigger item is the shipped memory/ folder: 175 AI-assistant notes including a personal profile, plus internal hostnames and cluster accounting in docs/. History also still holds pre-scrub profanity and colleague names.

### Findings

**OS1 [blocker, effort S] The shared lab install is world-writable: anyone who can write it runs code as every lab member**

Every file and directory of the lab install is mode 777, including drivers/, crboost, config/conf.yaml, config/qsub.sh, .uv/env/<hash>, .git and .claude/settings.local.json. The parent group dir is drwxrwxrwx root:root, an NFSv3 sec=sys mount with no ACL entries (getfacl shows user/group/other rwx). Servers run `<install>/.uv/env/.../python3 crboost.py` as each member. Their SLURM jobs run `export PYTHONPATH=<install>; <env python> <install>/drivers/x.py` (services/jobs/spec.py:565-573). conf.yaml chooses the container_path/bin_path every job executes, and qsub.sh is prepended to every job. So write access to the install means code execution as every member who starts a server or a job. Other users' writes are already visible: __pycache__ dirs owned by another lab account sit inside the install. The repo's own design makes it group-writable on purpose: `umask 002` in the launcher and install.sh, and 'A shared install is written by whoever starts it first'. That suits a trusted group, but a public install guide must not recommend it, and world-writable must never happen. Separately, a world-writable .claude/settings.local.json lets anyone add hooks to the Claude Code session of any member who starts it in that folder.

Evidence:
- ls -ld <lab install>/{.,.git,.uv,.uv/env/3.11.18-8febfdb40744,config/conf.yaml,config/qsub.sh,drivers/driver_base.py} -> all drwxrwxrwx / -rwxrwxrwx
- ls -ld /groups/<lab> -> drwxrwxrwx root root; getfacl -> other::rwx; mount -> nfs vers=3 sec=sys
- <lab install>/.claude/settings.local.json -rwxrwxrwx
- memory/reference_lab_install_rollout.md:16 ('Files are chmod 777 but core.filemode = false')
- crboost:26-28 (umask 002), install.sh:140-141 (umask 002, 'written by whoever starts it first'), install.sh:170 (chmod -R a+rX env)
- services/jobs/spec.py:565-573 (driver interpreter + PYTHONPATH from the install)

Recommendation: Today: `chmod -R go-w` on the lab install, make it owned by one maintainer account, and delete .claude/ from it. Ask IT whether the group dir really needs other::rwx. For the release, the installation guide should say: release dirs are owned by a site maintainer and read-only to users; only the maintainer builds envs (install.sh already handles a non-writable env root, install.sh:142-143); users' own state lives in ~/.crboost. Add a preflight warning when any file the server or jobs execute (drivers/, services/, config/qsub.sh, config/conf.yaml, the env) is world-writable, or group-writable by a group other than the expected one.

**OS2 [high, effort S] The public repo and any release archive ship AI-assistant memory, internal hosts and lab-specific docs**

memory/ is tracked: 175 files, 1.2 MB, about 7.5k lines of assistant working notes. That includes a personal profile of the maintainer (memory/user_<maintainer>.md) and preference notes, and 53 files name internal hosts or home/group paths. docs/ has 169 files, 87 of them with internal hostnames or lab paths. Examples: the cluster login FQDN, NFS server and storage server names (docs/roadmaps/completed/roadmap_artiax-bridge.md:1222,1707-1722); the OOD portal URL and a private IP (docs/roadmaps/roadmap_55-napari-companion-through-ondemand.md:51-52); institute-level fair-share standings of other institutes (docs/roadmaps/roadmap_45-gpu-utilisation.md:877-879). HEAD has 208 occurrences of /users/<maintainer> and 338 of /groups/<lab>, all in docs/memory/README; code and config templates are clean. The README opens with the lab's install path (README.md:12-16,78,89). container_defs carry Author labels with the PI's full name (4 defs, kept on purpose as credit) and placeholders 'Gemini', 'YourName', 'User' (relion5.0_tomo.def:5, imod.def:130, pymol.def:5, isonet2.def:48). There is no .gitattributes, so `git archive` and GitHub's release tarballs include all of it.

Evidence:
- git ls-files memory | wc -l -> 175; du -sh memory docs -> 1.2M 4.5M
- git grep -l (cbe|clip-|/groups/<lab>|/users/<maintainer>) HEAD -- memory | wc -l -> 53; -- docs -> 87
- README.md:12-16
- docs/roadmaps/roadmap_45-gpu-utilisation.md:877-879
- docs/roadmaps/roadmap_55-napari-companion-through-ondemand.md:52
- container_defs/relion5.0_tomo.def:5 'Author Gemini'; imod.def:130, pymol.def:5 'Author YourName'; isonet2.def:48 'Author User'
- ls .gitattributes -> missing

Recommendation: Move memory/ out of main: to a private repo, or keep it outside the checkout and link ~/.claude/.../memory to it. Add .gitattributes `export-ignore` for memory/, docs/roadmaps/, docs/reports/, tests/ data and check_look_literals.json, so release archives carry only the product. Make the README generic and move the lab path to a site note. Fix the placeholder Author labels and add a CITATION.cff/AUTHORS for credit. Run one pass over docs/ for hostnames, IPs and cluster accounting before the release tag.

**OS3 [high, effort S] By default the server listens on every interface over plain HTTP**

With no --host, bind_listen opens a dual-stack socket on '::' (IPv4 0.0.0.0 as fallback). Every host on the cluster network can reach the token gate. The default banner tunnels to 127.0.0.1 and does not need this. Only the SshLoginHost route needs a non-loopback bind: `-L port:<headnode>:port <login alias>` (crboost.py:40). On that route the last hop, login node to headnode, is cleartext HTTP that carries the token cookie or ?token=. The gate itself is sound: hmac.compare_digest, 43-char token_urlsafe(32), files at 0600, websockets refused with 1008, an open redirect prevented by stripping leading slashes, and the 401 page names only the owner. A token sniffed on the cluster network gives full control of the owner's server: SLURM submissions and file reads across their loaded projects.

Evidence:
- services/port_survey.py:330-341 (host None -> '::' dual stack / '0.0.0.0')
- crboost.py:122-124 (--host default: every interface)
- crboost.py:37-44 (banner: 127.0.0.1 tunnel unless via)
- services/server_auth.py:48-61,127-128,149-154
- docs/roadmaps (roadmap 23 text) shows http://<headnode>:<port>/?token=... as a direct-access option

Recommendation: Bind 127.0.0.1 by default. Bind the headnode's cluster-internal interface (not ::) only when local.SshLoginHost is set, and say so in the banner. Drop direct http://<headnode>:port links from the docs. Leave TLS out of scope: per-user servers behind SSH tunnels are the design.

**OS4 [high, effort M] The pinned dependencies carry 85 known advisories; GitHub's dependency graph seems off, so Dependabot shows none**

`uvx pip-audit -r requirements.txt --no-deps --disable-pip` (OSV/PyPI data, 2026-11-19) reported 124 records, 85 unique, in 5 of 78 pins. Pillow 12.0.0: 61, fixed by 13.0.0. NiceGUI 3.0.3: 14, all fixed by 3.12.0, among them CVE-2025-66645 (add_media_files traversal; not used here), XSS in ui.markdown, interactive_image and add_css, the eval() fallback in run_method (CVE-2026-27156), and component-asset dir handling (CVE-2026-45554). Starlette 0.48.0, pinned by fastapi 0.118.0: 6, among them CVE-2025-62727, a quadratic Range-header DoS in FileResponse, which this app uses for every raw-file route. requests 2.31.0: 3 (CVE-2024-35195, CVE-2024-47081 netrc leak, CVE-2026-25645). biopython 1.86: 1 (Entrez XXE; Entrez not used). Nearly everything is post-auth: the TokenGate wraps the whole ASGI app. XSS-by-data matters in a lab because projects are shared group-writable. Spot checks found escaping where it matters: log_html escapes (ui/components/log_pane.py:49-62) and ui.markdown renders static text. Still, 93 ui.html(..., sanitize=False) calls exist and only some modules escape. numpy 1.26.2, pandas 2.1.3 and scipy 1.11.4 are pinned on purpose to the cluster's 2023 module versions and carry no advisories. On GitHub, vulnerability alerts are enabled (204), yet there are 0 alerts and the SBOM endpoint returns 404, so the dependency graph is likely disabled.

Evidence:
- requirements.txt (nicegui==3.0.3, fastapi==0.118.0, starlette==0.48.0, pillow==12.0.0, requests==2.31.0)
- uvx pip-audit ... -> 'Found 124 known vulnerabilities in 5 packages' (scratchpad recon/ops_security/pip_audit.json)
- server_app.py:74,97,107,127 and ui/template_workbench.py:124 (FileResponse routes)
- git grep 'sanitize=False' -> 93
- gh api repos/<org>/<repo>/dependabot/alerts -> 0; .../dependency-graph/sbom -> 404

Recommendation: Before the December tag: requests>=2.33, Pillow>=13, fastapi to a release on starlette>=0.49.1 (better: starlette>=1.3.1 if NiceGUI allows), NiceGUI to >=3.12 with one browser pass over the views; NiceGUI 3.0->3.12 is the regression risk. Enable the dependency graph and Dependabot security updates. Add a weekly CI job running `uvx pip-audit -r requirements.txt --no-deps --disable-pip` that fails on new advisories. Add one CI grep listing sanitize=False calls whose argument is not a constant SVG or does not go through html.escape.

**OS5 [high, effort M] Updating with `git pull` under running servers mixes versions; tagged release dirs plus a `current` symlink fit the design with three small changes**

Today every site, the lab included, updates with `git pull` in place (README.md:124, docs/installation.md:216-227, the lab reflog shows 'pull --ff-only origin main' on 2026-11-19). Code then changes under a running server in several ways. (a) 475 function-local imports of repo modules in 90 server files load new code into an old process. (b) Spawned worker processes (services/workers.py) re-import from disk. (c) Queued SLURM jobs run whatever drivers/ and services/ hold when they start (roadmap 39 §6 item 2, not built). (d) static/ JS changes under an old server. Releases don't exist yet: 0 tags, and code_version() reports only branch@sha (services/code_version.py:47-61). Side-by-side release dirs already work with the current code in large part. crboost:14 and install.sh:19 resolve `pwd -P`, so a server started through /site/crboost/current runs from releases/vX. REPO_ROOT and sys.executable are resolved paths and get baked into each job's command (services/jobs/spec.py:565-573), so queued jobs keep the release that submitted them. That closes §6 item 2 with no code, as long as a release dir is not deleted while jobs reference it. Three gaps remain. (1) Port and server identity are keyed on the resolved install path: _preferred_port and ports.json (services/port_survey.py:361-371) and running_here (services/port_survey.py:120-123). Every release would move each user's port, breaking bookmarks and tunnel commands. An old release's server would go unnoticed, and two servers of one user would reconcile the same pipeline_active projects. (2) uv home is per checkout: UV_CACHE_DIR, UV_PYTHON_INSTALL_DIR and the uv binary are hardcoded to $here/.uv (install.sh:26-27,44), so each release downloads Python and wheels again. CRBOOST_ENV_ROOT can share envs, but `clean` refuses it (install.sh:77-78). (3) Site config lives inside the checkout: DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = REPO_ROOT/config/conf.yaml (services/configs/config_service.py:31), QSUB_FILE (preflight.py:22) and filterTilts/*.pth. Today it is synced by hand; the lab keeps 7 conf.yaml.bak-* copies. Read-only release dirs also mean drivers cannot write __pycache__, which costs about 0.6 s per task (roadmap 39 §6 item 1). On first start against old projects, minor schema bumps upgrade on save. v1->v2->v3 migrate with one-time .bak copies (services/project_state.py:1335-1355). A newer file opened by older code warns and keeps project_params.json.schema-X.Y.bak (services/project_state.py:1315-1325,1210-1219). Rollback is therefore safe within major 3.

Evidence:
- crboost:14, install.sh:19 (pwd -P)
- services/port_survey.py:120-123,361-371
- install.sh:26-27,44,77-78
- services/configs/config_service.py:31,36
- preflight.py:22
- services/jobs/spec.py:565-573
- git grep '^\s{4,}from (services|ui|api|drivers|backend)' -> 475 lines in 90 server files
- docs/roadmaps/roadmap_39-python-environment-and-setup.md §6 items 1-3
- <lab install>/.git/logs/HEAD (pull --ff-only origin main)
- <lab install>/config/conf.yaml.bak-{2026-10-02,...,20261118} (7 files)

Recommendation: Site layout: <root>/releases/vX.Y.Z/ (a `git clone --depth 1 --branch vX.Y.Z`, or `git worktree add`, owned by the site maintainer and read-only to users), <root>/current -> releases/vX.Y.Z, <root>/site/{conf.yaml, qsub.sh, weights/} and <root>/uv/ (shared python, cache and env). Users run <root>/current/crboost. Upgrade: clone the tag, symlink config/conf.yaml, config/qsub.sh and filterTilts/*.pth to site/, run install.sh, `./crboost python -m compileall -q .`, `./crboost preflight` and `./crboost test -m cluster`, then `ln -sfn` current. Users restart whenever they like; running servers and queued jobs keep their release. Rollback: point current back and restart; the schema .bak logic covers the data. Keep the last 3 releases and delete older ones only when no server or job uses them. Code: key port and running_here on the unresolved launcher dir (pass `pwd -L` from crboost as CRBOOST_INSTALL); add a CRBOOST_UV_HOME override; let preflight diff site conf.yaml against the template; make code_version() print the tag pointing at HEAD (refs/tags + packed-refs) so saved_by and run.out read 'v1.2.0@abc12345'. Keep `git pull` on main only for dev checkouts; production lab servers move to releases too.

**OS6 [medium, effort M] Users on the same compute node can reach curation/napari remote desktops**

The ChimeraX curation session starts `remotecontrol rest start port 46000+N`. Its REST server is on the node's loopback with no auth (the script's own comment says 'has no auth, so it's reachable only on-node'). But loopback on a compute node is shared by every user with a job there. Any of them can POST ChimeraX commands, Python included, into the session, which runs as its owner. The VNC desktop uses an 8-character VncAuth password, the protocol's maximum. Without OOD, Xvnc listens on all node interfaces (curation_session.sh:68-69). The password is written into session.json in <project>/.curation_sessions/<id>/ (session_service.py:125-128; curation_session.sh:258-260), which is group-readable because of umask 002, so group members can read it. Napari sessions live under ~/.crboost/napari, created 775; that is safe only where homes are 0700, as here. conf.template.yaml:281 offers passwordless_vnc (SecurityTypes None).

Evidence:
- containers/chimerax_artiax/curation_session.sh:108-118
- containers/chimerax_artiax/curation_session.sh:200-226,258-260
- services/curation/session_service.py:121-128
- config/conf.template.yaml:281
- ls -ld ~/.crboost/napari -> drwxrwxr-x

Recommendation: Write session.json with mode 600 and its dir 700: keep sessions under ~/.crboost or chmod the per-session dir inside the project. Replace the ChimeraX REST channel with a command file in that 0700 dir, polled by a small ChimeraX script, or front REST with a per-session secret. Bind Xvnc to localhost and tunnel to the node by ssh with -L to localhost (as the OOD route already does), and drop passwordless_vnc from the public template, or mark it clearly. Say plainly in docs/ that shared projects make group members trusted.

**OS7 [medium, effort S] History holds embarrassing but non-secret content; no rewrite is warranted**

Scan of `git log -p --all` (924 commits, 59 MB patch). Token patterns: 0 ghp_/github_pat_/gho_/sk-ant-/AKIA/private-key/xox/AIza/hf_. All 69 sk- hits are 'sk-status-state-machine'. Every ?token= example is truncated ('Xq3…'). The live token and secret (~/.crboost/token, ~/.crboost/secret) match nothing in history or the working tree, so the token pasted in chat never reached the repo. Historical config/conf.yaml and __config_safe/conf.yaml held only paths. .abacusai/config.json held only a permission list. Personal or embarrassing content does exist: 68 lines with profanity (HEAD has 0); pre-scrub colleague names (db53206 replaced 58 lines with 'the PI'/'a colleague'; the old lines stay in history); the PI's and the maintainer's institutional emails in old container labels and doc 'HeadNode' lines (11 lines); a colleague's /users home path (12 lines); a 1 MB desktop screenshot image-1.png (added 77562e3, removed 48387e7) showing an editor with a prompt file; internal hostnames; one private IP, also in HEAD. Commit authorship shows the maintainer's personal email on 897 commits, which is normal for git. The repo has 0 forks and 1 star, but 24 PRs. GitHub keeps refs/pull/* and cached views after a force push, and removing them takes GitHub Support, which helps only when rotating credentials can't fix the problem.

Evidence:
- grep -cE 'ghp_|github_pat_|AKIA|BEGIN .*PRIVATE KEY' history.patch -> 0
- grep -cF <live token> history.patch -> 0 (token and secret)
- grep -ciE profanity history.patch -> 68; git grep HEAD -> 0
- git show db53206 | grep -c '^+.*(the PI|a colleague)' -> 58
- git log -- image-1.png -> 77562e3 add, 48387e7 'remove junk'
- gh api .../pulls?state=all -> 24; .../forks -> 0
- https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/removing-sensitive-data-from-a-repository (PR refs read-only; Support needed)

Recommendation: Don't rewrite: nothing to rotate, PR refs would keep the old commits, and every lab clone and worktree would have to re-clone. Treat history as already public. Scrub HEAD (OS2). Turn on secret scanning and push protection (free for public repos) and add gitleaks to CI so nothing new gets in. If a 'clean slate' is wanted for optics, that is a new repo for the release line, which is the maintainer's call (open question 2).

**OS8 [medium, effort S] GitHub repo protections are all off**

Secret scanning, push protection, non-provider patterns and validity checks: disabled. Dependabot security updates: disabled. Private vulnerability reporting: disabled. main is not protected (anyone with write can force-push, and nothing has to pass before merge). No SECURITY.md, no .github/ (0 workflows, as known).

Evidence:
- gh api repos/<org>/<repo> -> security_and_analysis.* status disabled
- gh api .../branches/main/protection -> 404 Branch not protected
- gh api .../private-vulnerability-reporting -> {enabled:false}
- ls SECURITY.md .github -> missing

Recommendation: Enable secret scanning plus push protection, the dependency graph, Dependabot alerts and security updates (pip ecosystem; group the updates monthly), and private vulnerability reporting. Add SECURITY.md (supported release = latest tag; report via the GitHub advisory form). Protect main: no force-push; require the S4 lint workflow and pip-audit. Protect tags v* from deletion with a ruleset.

**OS9 [medium, effort S] install.sh supply chain: version-pinned but not hash-pinned**

uv: `curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/$want/install.sh | sh` (install.sh:57), with `|| true`, then only a `uv --version` string check (install.sh:63). The installer script itself is not pinned. I fetched it to the scratchpad without running it: it embeds sha256 values for the 0.13.7 archives (lines 230-343), verifies them (verify_checksum, lines 2114-2129) but skips silently when sha256sum is missing, and downloads from releases.astral.sh with GitHub releases as fallback (line 39). Python: `uv python install` (python-build-standalone); uv checks managed downloads against its embedded sha256 metadata (likely; not verified here). Packages: `uv pip sync requirements.txt` with versions pinned but no hashes (no --generate-hashes at install.sh:127-128), so a substituted or yanked-and-replaced artifact on the index would not be caught. only-binary=:all: (uv.toml) is good. UV_INDEX_URL and other uv env vars are honoured on purpose (install.sh:17), which helps sites with mirrors. The MCP forwarder resolves `mcp>=2.3,<3` and `httpx>=0.28,<1` from PyPI on each user's first run, with no lock (agent/crboost_mcp.py:1-5).

Evidence:
- install.sh:48-66
- install.sh:127-128,156-158
- uv.toml:5-17
- agent/crboost_mcp.py:1-5
- scratchpad recon/ops_security/dl_uvinstaller/install.sh:39,230-343,517,2114-2129

Recommendation: Compile with `--generate-hashes`; uv pip sync then enforces them. Keep requirements.txt, since hashes need no pyproject. Pin the uv archive's sha256 in install.sh, download the archive directly (or run the installer with sha256sum required), and drop `|| true`. Run `uv lock --script agent/crboost_mcp.py` to commit the forwarder's lock. CI: a job that runs install.sh on ubuntu-latest from a fresh clone proves the chain end to end.

**OS10 [medium, effort M] Container recipes: TLS checks disabled and unpinned installers (brief; the containers lens covers the rest)**

imod.def downloads with `wget --no-check-certificate` (lines 39, 54). About 9 defs fetch Miniconda3-latest or Miniforge3 'latest' (e.g. cryocare.def:22, isonet2.def:15, relion-5.0.1-...sm100.def:75). Base images use tags, not digests. pymol.def:44-47 runs pip install with no versions. Images built from the same .def at different times will differ, and a TLS man-in-the-middle could swap the IMOD installer.

Evidence:
- container_defs/imod.def:39,54
- git grep 'Miniconda3-latest|Miniforge3' container_defs -> 9+ defs
- container_defs/pymol.def:44-47

Recommendation: Remove --no-check-certificate. Pin installers by version and sha256, and base images by digest, in the defs the release names. This ties into roadmap 39 S5 (%labels) and the container-serving plan.

**OS11 [low, effort S] Every page loads fonts from Google (IP to a third party; breaks offline sites)**

server_app.py:135-141, ui/main_ui.py:41-42 and ui/dashboard/css.py:2108 load IBM Plex from fonts.googleapis.com and fonts.gstatic.com in the user's browser. In the EU the LG München I judgment (20 Jan 2022, 3 O 17493/20) held that remote Google Fonts pass the visitor's IP to Google unlawfully, and it set off a wave of warning letters. A German partner site's data-protection officer may well raise this. On an air-gapped or firewalled cluster the fonts fail, and the house look falls back to system fonts.

Evidence:
- server_app.py:135-141
- ui/main_ui.py:41-42
- ui/dashboard/css.py:2108
- https://www.gesetze-bayern.de/Content/Document/Y-300-Z-GRURRS-B-2022-N-612?view=Print (LG München I, 3 O 17493/20)

Recommendation: Vendor IBM Plex Sans, Mono and Sans Condensed (SIL OFL) under static/fonts and serve them with @font-face from /static.

**OS12 [low, effort S] Token handling details: the cookie is the token, never rotated, shared by every localhost port**

The gate's cookie value is the raw token (server_auth.py:154: HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; no Secure; no Max-Age). Cookies are scoped by host, not port. As the code notes (server_auth.py:29-31), any other server the user opens at http://localhost:<other port> gets crboost_token_<port> in its request headers: a colleague's server reached by tunnel, Jupyter, a dev server. The token never rotates; the only way is to delete ~/.crboost/token and restart (server_auth.py:5-7). The agent API already refuses cookie auth for writes (api/router.py:82-86). The MCP forwarder sends the bearer only to a listener owned by the user's uid (agent/crboost_mcp.py:13-16,140-158). GET /api/snapshot/plan has side effects (rmtree .capture-*, mkdir; ui/snapshot/routes.py:76-94) and is reachable by a top-level cross-site navigation carrying the Lax cookie, provided the attacker guesses the port (32-port range) and the project name. Impact is minor. NiceGUI's socket.io accepts any origin (cors_allowed_origins='*', nicegui.py:51), so the cookie is the only websocket guard. ~/.crboost is created with no explicit mode (server_auth.py:50) under umask 002; its logs land at 664 (~/.crboost/logs, server_auth/log_setup), which is safe only where homes are 0700.

Evidence:
- services/server_auth.py:5-7,29-31,50,149-154
- api/router.py:82-86
- agent/crboost_mcp.py:13-16,140-158
- ui/snapshot/routes.py:76-94
- .uv/env/.../site-packages/nicegui/nicegui.py:51
- services/log_setup.py:29,239

Recommendation: Make the cookie value an HMAC of the token with the port (rotatable through the storage secret), not the token itself. Add `./crboost token --rotate`. Create ~/.crboost with 0700 and log files with 0600. Make snapshot/plan a POST. Document 'a ?token= link is a password'.

**OS13 [low, effort S] No routine project_params.json backup across code versions**

Saves are atomic (mkstemp + rename, services/project_state.py:1199-1236). Backups exist only for v1/v2 migrations and when older code saves a file a newer schema wrote. A release that changes meaning within the same schema minor has no fallback. If the species or feature registry fails validation on load, it is reset with a load_warning, and the next save persists the empty registry (services/project_state.py:1382-1393). That is the likely failure mode of version skew during a rollback.

Evidence:
- services/project_state.py:1199-1236
- services/project_state.py:1335-1355
- services/project_state.py:1382-1393

Recommendation: On the first save where saved_by differs from the loaded file's saved_by, copy the old file to project_params.json.<old saved_by>.bak (one per version, bounded). With OS5's tag-bearing code_version, a site rollback then has a matching file to go back to.

**OS14 [low, effort S] Shell scripts are mostly clean; qsub template's `cd` can fail silently**

shellcheck 0.x (via uvx shellcheck-py) over crboost, install.sh, config/qsub.template.sh and the 3 session scripts found 7 findings and 0 errors. The one that matters is SC2164 at config/qsub.template.sh:42: `cd "${JOB_DIR}"` has no `|| exit`. On a node where the project mount is missing, the job runs in the wrong cwd and touches RELION_JOB_EXIT_* there instead of failing clearly. Others: SC2154 rc unassigned (curation_session.sh:34), SC2086/2016/2155/2012 notes. Separately, the driver launch does not shell-quote the interpreter or PYTHONPATH (services/jobs/spec.py:573), so an install path with spaces breaks every job.

Evidence:
- uvx --from shellcheck-py shellcheck ... (scratchpad recon/ops_security/shellcheck.txt)
- config/qsub.template.sh:42
- services/jobs/spec.py:573

Recommendation: Use `cd "${JOB_DIR}" || exit 1` in the template; preflight already checks qsub.sh structure and can check this. shlex.quote the paths in driver_launch_prefix. Add shellcheck to the S4 CI workflow.

### Facts

- No live credential (GitHub PAT, OpenAI/Anthropic key, AWS key, private key, Slack/Google/HF token) appears anywhere in git history (924 commits, all refs). (verified; git log -p --all > history.patch; grep -E patterns -> 0 (sk- hits are 'sk-status-state-machine'))
- The maintainer's current server token and session secret appear in neither history nor the working tree; every ?token= example in docs is truncated ('Xq3…'). (verified; grep -cF $(cat ~/.crboost/token) history.patch -> 0; grep -rlF in worktree -> 0)
- The GitHub repo has 24 PRs, 0 forks, 1 star; secret scanning, push protection, Dependabot security updates and private vulnerability reporting are disabled; main is unprotected. (verified; gh api repos/<org>/<repo>, .../pulls, .../forks, .../branches/main/protection, .../private-vulnerability-reporting)
- Every HTTP and websocket scope passes through TokenGate before NiceGUI, FastAPI or /static; the agent API's write verbs need the Bearer header. (verified; server_app.py:185-193; services/server_auth.py:70-125; api/router.py:82-86)
- Raw-file routes and their guards: /api/file (.mrc/.map/.rec/.ccp4 inside a loaded project or a registered template/mask), /api/tilt-thumb (.png in loaded project), /api/vis-asset (.png/.json in loaded project), /api/project-preview (only */warp_tiltseries/reconstruction/*.png under any dir that has a project_params.json ancestor, loaded or not), /api/snapshot (.zip in a loaded project's Snapshots/), /api/kitchen/* (loaded pool + regex names), /api/bm-pic (in-memory dict), /static (repo static/), /snapshot/capture(.js) (fixed files). (verified; ui/template_workbench.py:113-124; server_app.py:64-128,130; ui/snapshot/routes.py:66-161; ui/sta_kitchen/routes.py:28-112; ui/branch_map/facts.py:47-53; services/project_state.py:1621-1635)
- Without --host the server binds '::' dual-stack (all interfaces), falling back to 0.0.0.0; ports 24300-24331. (verified; services/port_survey.py:35,330-341; crboost.py:122-124)
- A server started through a symlinked install path runs from the resolved path, and the jobs it submits use the resolved interpreter and REPO_ROOT. (verified; crboost:14 and install.sh:19 (pwd -P); services/configs/config_service.py:30 (resolve()); services/jobs/spec.py:565-573 (sys.executable))
- Preferred port, ports.json and 'already running' detection are keyed on the resolved install path. (verified; services/port_survey.py:120-123,361-382)
- The lab install is updated by `git pull --ff-only origin main` (last on 2026-11-19, to 80a874b) and all its files are mode 777. (verified; <lab install>/.git/logs/HEAD; ls -la <lab install>)
- pip-audit on requirements.txt: 124 records, 85 unique advisories in 5 of 78 packages (pillow 61, nicegui 14, starlette 6, requests 3, biopython 1); fixes at pillow 13.0.0, nicegui 3.12.0, starlette 1.3.1 (0.49.1 for the FileResponse DoS), requests 2.33.0. (verified; uvx pip-audit -r requirements.txt --no-deps --disable-pip -f json (2026-11-19))
- NiceGUI 3.0.3 creates its socket.io server with cors_allowed_origins='*'. (verified; .uv/env/3.11.18-8febfdb40744/lib/python3.11/site-packages/nicegui/nicegui.py:51)
- The uv 0.13.7 installer embeds per-archive sha256 values and checks them when sha256sum exists, and skips the check silently otherwise. (verified; https://astral.sh/uv/0.13.7/install.sh lines 39, 230-343, 2114-2129 (fetched to scratchpad, not run))
- uv verifies sha256 of managed Python downloads against its built-in metadata. (likely; general knowledge of uv's python-downloads metadata; not checked in this session)
- After a history rewrite GitHub keeps PR refs and cached views; full removal needs GitHub Support. (verified; https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/removing-sensitive-data-from-a-repository)
- Remote Google Fonts were held unlawful (IP transfer without consent) by LG München I on 20 Jan 2022 (3 O 17493/20); a first-instance decision. (verified; https://www.gesetze-bayern.de/Content/Document/Y-300-Z-GRURRS-B-2022-N-612?view=Print)
- Per-user config overrides go to ~/.crboost/conf.yaml; the server's UI never writes the install's config/conf.yaml. (verified; services/configs/config_service.py:3-7,505-525)
- Server logs: ~/.crboost/logs/crboost-<port>.log, 5 x 20 MB rotating, one file set per port; drivers print '[DRIVER] crboost <code_version> from <REPO_ROOT>' to each job's run.out. (verified; services/log_setup.py:8,21,29; drivers/driver_base.py:55)
- Schema: (3,14); minor differences upgrade on save; v1/v2 migrate with one-time .bak; a newer-on-disk file opened by older code warns and keeps project_params.json.schema-X.Y.bak on first save; there are no other migrations. (verified; services/project_state.py:42-64,1290-1355,1210-1219)
- The ChimeraX curation session's REST control port is unauthenticated on the node's loopback. (verified; containers/chimerax_artiax/curation_session.sh:110-118 (comment: 'has no auth'))
- Another account on the cluster can actually write the lab install (NFS server-side ACLs could still restrict it). (likely; mode bits 777 and getfacl other::rwx on NFSv3 sec=sys; not tested with another account)
- GitHub's dependency graph is off for the repo, which is why Dependabot shows 0 alerts despite pip-audit's 85. (likely; gh api .../dependency-graph/sbom -> 404; .../dependabot/alerts -> 0; .../vulnerability-alerts -> 204)
- Git history contains 68 profanity lines, pre-scrub colleague names (scrub commit db53206), two institutional emails, a colleague's home path and a desktop screenshot (image-1.png); HEAD contains none of the profanity. (verified; grep over history.patch; git show db53206; git log -- image-1.png)

### Open questions

- Keep memory/ (assistant notes, 175 files incl. a personal profile) in the public repo? Recommended: no. Move it to a private repo or a folder outside the checkout before the release tag, and export-ignore docs/roadmaps and docs/reports from release archives.
- Rewrite history to drop the pre-scrub names, profanity and screenshot? Recommended: no. No secret is involved, 24 PR refs would keep the commits on GitHub, and every clone would have to re-clone. Accept it and scrub HEAD.
- Default bind address: loopback unless local.SshLoginHost is set? Recommended: yes. The lab sets SshLoginHost because of its two login nodes; a single-login-node site gets 127.0.0.1.
- Release channel for sites: tagged release dirs + `current` symlink, or `git pull` on main? Recommended: release dirs for every production install, the lab's included. main stays for dev checkouts. Needs OS5's three code changes (install-keyed port, shared uv home, site config outside the checkout).
- Version scheme? Recommended: SemVer 0.y.z (or CalVer YYYY.M.patch), kept apart from the project schema version. The tag goes into code_version()/saved_by, and the December release is v1.0.0 only if the maintainer commits to schema compatibility within major 3.
- Upgrade NiceGUI 3.0.3 -> >=3.12 (and fastapi/starlette, Pillow, requests) before December, at the cost of a full browser pass? Recommended: yes, early in the six weeks. Each of those packages has known advisories at the pinned version.
- Who owns a site install? Recommended: one site-maintainer account owns releases/ and site/, users get read-only access, and only the maintainer runs install.sh. The docs say so, and preflight warns on world- or foreign-group-writable code.
- Container def credit: keep the PI's full name in Author labels or move credit to CITATION.cff/AUTHORS? Recommended: move it, and fix the placeholder labels ('Gemini', 'YourName', 'User').

## recon:prior_plans: the same brief, re-run on its own after the first session stopped

The record already holds most of a release plan, scattered across roadmaps 20, 23, 39 and 42, and almost none of its deployment half is built: roadmap 39 S4 (CI) and S5 (container labels + weights sha256) are scoped but unbuilt, roadmap 20 §1.7 (pin every latest/HEAD in the defs) is still open, roadmap 23 stage 6 (release install + update banner) is unbuilt, and roadmap 42 D6 (the Claude Code plugin 'later, for the lab') is unbuilt. The only prior notion of a release is 'the release install is a main clone updated with git pull' with 'no version string in the code' (roadmap 23 §3.8); the lab install did 17 fast-forward pulls of main this month, so main is today's release channel, and code_version() reads .git, so an archive install would stamp nothing. S4's CI recipe would fail on day one: ruff check has 2 errors (both in the recon dump script), ruff format --check flags 50 files, and §6 still says 'import main', which no longer exists. Container recipes have no single source: the lab runs *_sm100 defs plus easymode_1.2.5 and napari_GL_v2, easymode's def exists only on /groups, the repo's napari_GL.def is the lab's v2 under the v1 name, four repo defs differ from the lab copies, and installation.md tells a newcomer to build the five images the lab deleted on 2026-11-17. conf.template.yaml ships CBE.next values as live values (gpu:nvidia_b200:1, qos g_medium, curation partition c), contradicting roadmap 43 S2's 'stays generic'. Munich was promised an unmodified-checkout re-install from the README only (roadmap 20 S5). There is no record that it happened, and its R1 ask (conda) was later walked back to 'not supported'. The fresh-clone path (roadmap 39 §4, S6 user checks) has never been walked end to end, the DL tilt filter has never run on a project from its image, and HOTFIXES H3 (Cs 2.7 / amplitude contrast 0.10 hard-wired) is 'high on other hardware'. Many 'NOT RUN' labels sit on paths production has since used (liveness, agent API, TM S2), so before release the record needs a verdict pass that separates real unverified default paths from stale labels.

### Findings

**PP1 [high, effort S] (a) Roadmap 39 S4 (CI + pinned ruff) is scoped but not built, and its recipe fails on today's tree**

S4 plans .github/workflows/lint.yml on the free public-repo runners: install requirements.txt (ruff 0.15.12 is in the lock since S6/D13), then ruff check ., ruff format --check ., python check_boundaries.py, python -m compileall -q .; no pytest because tests/ is the install self-test and needs a site's conf.yaml. Its precondition 'fix the 6 E501 lines' is stale. **PP11 [high, effort S] (b) Python environment decisions (roadmap 39) the release plan must build on**

D1: lock = requirements.in compiled to a pinned requirements.txt (not pyproject/uv.lock). D2: no hashes in the lock (a hand-added package would fail the install); stands after S6. D4 revised 2026-11-06: uv, its CPython and its cache live inside each checkout (.uv/), so an install depends on nothing outside its folder. D6 revised: tilt filter torch 2.8.0/cu128; glibc >= 2.28 required, older nodes left out. D8-D13: the environment is a build product named <python>-<12 hex of sha256 over the pin lines>, built under a temp name and renamed, never modified; a lock change builds a new one beside the old; clean keeps this lock's and the newest other; torch lives only in the tilt_filter image; one lock including ruff. uv.toml pins uv (required-version ==0.13.7, wheels only, only-managed Python, compile-bytecode). In a checkout users cannot write to, ./crboost refuses and names install.sh. §5: if uv must go, the exit is plain venv + pip on the same requirements.txt (only the self-contained interpreter is lost).

**PP12 [high, effort S] (b) Container decisions already taken**

One container per tool whenever Python/TF/torch/CUDA pinnings differ (bundling rejected). A newer version of a tool upgrades its existing image and tools: key in place (no parallel key per job), then check every CLI crboost calls for changed options. Image labels warn, never block ('a hand-built image may be right'). The tilt filter's network runs only on a GPU in its own image; no CPU mode ('a fallback would hide a broken node'). Rebuilt *_sm100 images keep the old architectures, so one image serves CBE.next's B200s and older cards (the maintainer's memory). PTX-only CUDA-11/TF images are not rebuilt (no TF release ships sm_100 SASS); a shared gpu_kernel_cache absorbs their JIT. CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES is deliberately not forwarded into containers (pytom -g uses host ids).

**PP13 [high, effort S] (b) Serving and identity decisions (roadmaps 23, 52) that bound how the tool is deployed**

Per-person servers started by each user, identity = Unix user, Jupyter-style token in ~/.crboost/token with no opt-out flag, tokened link printed at start; no VM, hub, SSO, user DB or TLS 'not now' (front door parked, would spawn these same servers). Port block 24300-24331 remembered per checkout. Version skew between installs warns and backs up, never makes a project read-only and never stops jobs (maintainer amended §3.5). Never put a tokened URL in a bug report. Roadmap 52 D5: no chown without root; every project group-writable by default: the launcher sets umask 002 (inherited by sbatch jobs) and new project folders are g+rwxs.

**PP14 [high, effort S] (b) Site-portability rules (roadmap 20) and template policy (roadmaps 43, 47)**

Site values live in conf.yaml only (container_runtime, container_binds, tool entries, partitions); code defaults for partitions/constraints are empty and preflight fails by key name; an unconfigured tool raises (no guessed binaries, no 'empty path -> native'); misspelled keys are load warnings; the driver interpreter is the server's sys.executable; PYTHONPATH comes from the running server, not qsub.sh; the qsub exit-marker block is enforced byte for byte. Roadmap 43 S2: conf.template.yaml 'stays generic (gpu:1)' and names CBE.next types only in comments. Roadmap 47 D2 recommends keeping supervisor orchestration as the template default for sites that cannot keep a server up.

**PP15 [medium, effort S] (b) Process rules that shape a release workflow**

Never commit, stage, push, tag or branch for the maintainer: hand line-by-line commands, no Claude attribution. Build arcs back to back and verify once at the end (standing decision 2026-08-17). The repo is public and memory/ ships in it: roles not names, no quoted curses, also in paths and file names. No automation that works around cluster failures (static exclude + report to the admins). Never invent or silently default a value; surface it. Jobs are idempotent; a job never deletes another job's outputs. CI runs lint only, no pytest (tests need a site conf). Deliverables are repo markdown, never claude.ai Artifacts.

- The repo has no .github/ directory and GitHub reports 0 Actions workflows; 0 tags, 0 releases. (verified; ls .github; gh api repos/Klumpe-lab/crboost_server/actions/workflows; git tag)
- ruff 0.15.12 (the locked one): `ruff check .` finds 2 errors, both in docs/reports/public-release/dump_recon.py (E401 line 7, E741 line 35); `ruff format --check .` would reformat 50 files; check_boundaries.py is clean. (verified; read-only run with --no-cache from .uv/env/3.11.18-8febfdb40744 on 2026-11-19)
- main.py no longer exists; the entry chain is ./crboost (sh) -> crboost.py -> server_app.py. (verified; ls main.py; crboost:40; CLAUDE.md)
- The lab install /groups/<lab>/software/crboost_server is on main at 80a874b (one docs commit behind dev), updated by `git pull --ff-only origin main` 17 times since 2026-11-01 (51 pulls in its reflog). (verified; /groups/<lab>/software/crboost_server/.git/HEAD, refs/heads/main, logs/HEAD)
- The lab install runs env .uv/env/3.11.18-8febfdb40744 (same hash as dev) and still has a real venv/ directory and its own copy of filterTilts/tiltnet_resnet18_o1034.pth. (verified; ls /groups/<lab>/software/crboost_server/{.uv/env,venv,filterTilts})
- The lab conf.yaml points every tool at /groups/<lab>/software/containers/sifs/*: the *_sm100 warp, relion, pytom, isonet images, cryocare.sif, imod.sif, pymol.sif, miss_alignment, tilt_filter, easymode_1.2.5.sif, napari_GL_v2.sif, chimerax_artiax_GL.sif. (verified; /groups/<lab>/software/crboost_server/config/conf.yaml:297-410)
- 15 defs in container_defs/; 10 are byte-identical to the lab's /groups/<lab>/software/containers/defs copies, 4 differ (chimerax_artiax_GL, cryocare, imod, isonet2), and the repo's napari_GL.def equals the lab's napari_GL_v2.def. easymode_1.2.5.def, ais.def, pom_cryoet.def and others exist only in the lab dir. (verified; cmp over both directories, 2026-11-19)
- No container def has crboost.* %labels (roadmap 39 S5 not built). (verified; grep -l 'crboost\.' container_defs/*.def -> none)
- code_version() reads <branch>@<sha> from .git without a git binary and returns None for an unpacked release archive; it is stamped into every project save (saved_by) and printed by each supervisor at start. (verified; services/code_version.py:47-66; services/project_state.py:1208; drivers/driver_base.py:55)
- Data formats carry their own versions: project SCHEMA_VERSION (3, 14), registry (1, 8), protocol schema 1, cutout filters 2. (verified; services/project_state.py:64; services/tilt_series/registry.py:97; services/protocols/schema.py:31; services/visualization/cutout_filters.py:118)
- install.sh fetches the pinned uv with `curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/<ver>/install.sh | sh` (no checksum), then uv's own CPython (python-build-standalone from GitHub) and wheels from PyPI; mirrors via UV_INDEX_URL / UV_PYTHON_INSTALL_MIRROR / CRBOOST_UV are documented. (verified; install.sh:50-64; docs/installation.md:41-49)
- uv is pinned at ==0.13.7, Python at 3.11.18, wheels only, bytecode compiled at sync. (verified; uv.toml:5-20; .python-version)
- The MCP forwarder's dependencies are ranges (mcp>=2.3,<3; httpx>=0.28,<1) resolved by `uv run --script` at first start, with no script lock. (verified; agent/crboost_mcp.py:1-5; crboost:36-37)
- The ./crboost launcher sets umask 002 for the server and, through sbatch, its jobs. (verified; crboost:25-28)
- GitHub issues #114-#137 (roadmaps 19-48 and the unnumbered open roadmaps) were created 2026-11-10 by scripts/file_roadmap_issues.py, committed later as ab2af90 (2026-11-19); roadmaps 49-63 are not filed; 113 issues total, 0 milestones. (verified; gh issue list -R Klumpe-lab/crboost_server --state all; git log ab2af90)
- GitHub has 7 branches: main plus denovo_picking, isonet_auto_and_missalignment_fix, k3_implementation, missalignmnet, pipeline_state_expansion, protocols. (verified; gh api repos/Klumpe-lab/crboost_server/branches)
- The GitHub repo has issues, projects and wiki enabled, no topics, no homepage; description 'A headnode-centric implementation of cryoboost'. (verified; gh api repos/Klumpe-lab/crboost_server)
- memory/ (175 files) is tracked in the public repo, as are docs/reports (66 files). (verified; git ls-files memory | wc -l; git ls-files docs/reports | wc -l)

- The five superseded images (warp_2.0.0dev41_aretomo1.0.0_cuda12.9, warp_2.0.0dev41_cuda12.9, isonet2, relion5.0_tomo, pytom_match_pick_0.10.0) were deleted from the lab's sifs/ on 2026-11-17; their defs were kept. (verified; <assistant home>/memory/project_cbe_next_migration_cleanup.md; ls /groups/<lab>/software/containers/sifs)
- Legacy CBE shuts down 2026-12-18. (unverified; <assistant home>/memory/project_cbe_next_migration_cleanup.md:34)

- Commit velocity on main is high (about 25 commits on 2026-11-19 alone, 914 in total); main doubles as the lab's production channel. (verified; git log)

### Open questions

- Release channel: keep roadmap 23 §3.8 ('the release install is a main clone; no version string in the code') or move installs to tags? Recommended: tags cut from main by the maintainer (calendar-style, e.g. 2026.12.0), installs check out a tag, the version derived from git describe (and written into a VERSION file only for archive installs, since code_version() returns None without .git); main stays the dev branch the lab may still track.
- Single source for container recipes: is container_defs/ the only home (lab defs dir becomes a build cache), and does easymode_1.2.5.def go into the repo? Recommended: yes to both, after checking easymode's licence; delete the four superseded defs from the tree.
- How Munich gets images: build from the pinned defs themselves, or pull prebuilt SIFs the lab publishes (e.g. ORAS to GHCR, by digest)? Recommended: publish prebuilt, labelled SIFs per release and keep 'build from defs' as the documented fallback. Building on their site doubles the flaky-build cost the container-separation decision already pays.
- D7: may the tilt-filter weights be published (model author's licence), and where? Recommended: Hugging Face with a sha256 in the template; until then the template ships `tilt_filter: {models: {}}`.
- Conda for Munich (their review item R1): reopen, or confirm 'not supported'? Recommended: confirm not supported and say so in the handoff; the 'start crboost.py with your own Python' route stays as is.
- CBE.next values in conf.template.yaml (gpu:nvidia_b200:1, g_medium, partition c): move to comments as roadmap 43 S2 intended? Recommended: yes.
- Ship the Claude Code plugin (roadmap 42 D6 'later, for the lab') in the first release? Recommended: yes, with a script-locked forwarder, since the README front page advertises the feature.
- H3/H2/H4 before the Munich handoff: what are Munich's microscope Cs, amplitude contrast and handedness? Recommended: ask now; fix H3 before release if their Cs is not 2.7 mm.
- What may a release say about NOT RUN features: block, flag as experimental, or hide? Recommended: one triage pass (PP24), with default-path items blocking and opt-in ones flagged.
- Keep mirroring roadmaps as GitHub issues (15 unfiled), and rename the name-bearing issue titles? Recommended: rename now; mirror only roadmaps that a release milestone includes.
- Should queued jobs be protected from an install update (roadmap 39 §6 item 2)? Recommended: yes. Make immutable per-tag install directories part of the release layout, or at least add the submit-time code_version stamp.
